# Household hh_s18 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: couple. Rooms: balcony, bathroom, bedroom_1, entry, kitchen, living, office.

Residents:

- **Ines** (resident_1): works from home; very tidy (tidiness 0.77); average timing (jitter scale 0.97); rarely forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.78); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the office desk. Hobbies: music, snacking. Chores they take on: dusting, fixing, grocery_run, vacuuming.
- **Elena** (resident_2): works outside the home; untidy (tidiness 0.46); punctual (jitter scale 0.73); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood barely affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.30); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: board_games, yoga. Chores they take on: fixing, grocery_run, vacuuming.

## Day 15 — Tuesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d15`: Elena hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:34–16:26).
- `ep:deadline:resident_2:d15:looming`: Elena has a deadline tomorrow at noon: works into the evening, eats fast with the laptop at the table (18:00–24:00).
- `ep:early_night:resident_1:d15`: Ines turns in early tonight: a short evening, bed 45 minutes sooner (20:30–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_1:d15`: Ines is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (15:23–17:23).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Ines): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Ines has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Ines energy 0.00, hurriedness 0.17, distraction 0.71; Elena energy 0.34, hurriedness 0.67, distraction 0.56

## Day 27 — Sunday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d27`: Elena is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:37–16:07).
- `ep:deadline:resident_2:d27:looming`: Elena has a deadline tomorrow at noon: works into the evening, eats fast with the laptop at the table (18:00–24:00).
- `distracted:resident_1`: Ines is distracted (0.85): carries things into the next room absent-mindedly, more likely to forget pocket items.
